In clinical settings, interventions are documented as bundles: a co-occurrence audit of 945,707 patient-hours from MIMIC-IV shows groups of components, such as every parameter of a dialysis circuit, that never appear apart, so an edit that changes one component on its own describes an hour that never occurs in the data. We hypothesize that the granularity at which an intervention is edited changes how a world model responds, and test this with Clin-JEPA, a latent world model of patient trajectories conditioned on hourly treatment text. At 1,019 documented onsets of invasive ventilation, we keep the patient's history and other treatments fixed and compare editing one ventilator setting with editing the complete configuration recorded for a real patient with the most similar recent trajectory. The complete bundle moves the predicted next state further than any single setting, consistently across all five settings, and the difference remains after accounting for how much each edit changes the model's input. Intervention granularity therefore materially affects the response of a clinical world model: single-component edits may understate treatment sensitivity, and bundle-aware editing may offer a better-supported basis for counterfactual treatment simulation.

</details>


### [213] [Beyond Kinematics: Benchmarking Simulation Fidelity for Muscle-Driven Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21909)
*Ayah G. Ahmad,Claire E. Borden,Maegan Tucker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统对比SCONE/HyFyDy与MuJoCo/MyoSim两种动作模仿强化学习流程：两者运动学精度相近，但HyFyDy的肌肉激活更贴合实验EMG，更适合肌肉骨骼建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIRL流程虽能高保真复现人体运动学，但是否能准确捕捉产生动作的神经肌肉行为尚不明确。这一问题对机器人辅助设备的设计与控制尤为关键，因为肌肉激活模式和代谢消耗常被用作优化目标。

Method: 选取基于SCONE/HyFyDy（强调详细肌肉-肌腱建模的生理真实性）与基于MuJoCo/MyoSim（强调计算效率与可扩展策略学习）的两种最新MIRL流程，使用同一套人体动作捕捉与肌电（EMG）测量数据进行系统对比。比较维度覆盖运动学精度与肌肉激活与实验EMG的一致性，采用平均池化后的RMSE与相关系数r作为指标。

Result: 两种流程均能以相对较高精度产生相近的运动学结果；但在肌肉激活方面，HyFyDy与实验EMG更一致，其平均池化（RMSE, r）为（0.164, 0.4），MuJoCo为（0.344, 0.11）。

Conclusion: HyFyDy当前更先进的生理真实性使其更适合肌肉骨骼建模；两者均需进一步发展，以将生理真实性引入GPU并行仿真环境，从而推进机器人辅助设备设计。

Abstract: In this work, we conduct a systematic comparison of two state-of-the-art motion-imitation reinforcement learning (MIRL) pipelines, one built on SCONE/HyFyDy and one built on MuJoCo/MyoSim. HyFyDy emphasizes physiological realism through detailed musculotendon modeling, while MuJoCo prioritizes computational efficiency and scalable policy learning. While recent work has demonstrated that both pipelines reproduce human kinematics with high fidelity, it remains unclear if they accurately capture the underlying neuromuscular behavior that produced the movement. This limitation is particularly important for robotic assistive-device design and control, where outcome measures such as muscle activation patterns and metabolic cost are often used as optimization targets. To conduct a systematic comparison, our work compares both pipelines using a common set of human motion-capture and electromyography (EMG) measurements. The results find that while both pipelines produce similar kinematics with relative accuracy, the muscle activations from HyFyDy are more aligned with the experimental EMG, as supported by the average pooled (RMSE, r) values for muscle activations from HyFyDy and MuJoCo: (0.164, 0.4) and (0.344, 0.11), respectively. While we conclude that the more advanced physiological realism of HyFyDy currently makes it more suitable for musculoskeletal modeling, both require further development to bring physiological realism to GPU-parallelizable simulation environments and advance robotic assistive device design.

</details>


### [214] [Kinks vs. Smoothness: Identifiability of Real Analytic nICA for Laplace-like Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21926)
*Isaac Manring,Kejun Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在生成函数为实解析函数、且源概率密度函数的一阶导数仅有有限个不连续点时，非线性独立成分分析（nICA）能够实现可辨识性（精确恢复，仅剩平凡歧义）；Laplace分布是满足该假设的典型例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性独立成分分析的核心挑战是在非线性和隐藏混合的情况下恢复真实的独立潜在因子，而非其某种混淆版本。已有可辨识性结果通常依赖较强的分布或生成函数假设，限制了适用范围，因此需要更宽松且能与现有训练流程兼容的条件。

Method: 作者利用源分布中的“拐点”（kinks，即一阶导数不连续处）与实解析函数的平滑性之间的对比来构造证明，证明在上述假设下可实现精确恢复（仅差平凡歧义）。实解析函数可由带标准激活函数（tanh、softplus、GELU）的Normalizing Flows或变分自编码器（VAE）近似，因此方法可基本无改动地接入现有训练管线。并在真实与合成数据上使用Normalizing Flows和VAE进行实验验证。

Result: 理论和实验均表明，在给定假设下Normalizing Flows与VAE具备可辨识性，能够恢复真实潜在因子。在CelebA数据实验中，成功恢复了若干可解释的潜在因子，这些因子对应数据集中独特的属性。

Conclusion: 论文为非线性独立成分分析提供了一个适用范围较广的可辨识性结果：只要生成函数为实解析且源分布一阶导数仅有有限个不连续点（如Laplace分布），即可实现精确恢复；该结论可直接应用于常见生成模型，并在真实数据上获得可解释的潜在表示。

Abstract: Many machine learning systems try to explain complex data - like images or financial time series - in terms of hidden, independent factors that generated them. Recovering the true underlying factors, rather than some scrambled version of them, is the central challenge of nonlinear Independent Component Analysis (nICA). We prove identifiability (exact recovery) up to trivial ambiguities for real analytic generating functions when source probability density functions have a finite number of discontinuities in the first derivative. The Laplace distribution is the most prominent example satisfying this assumption. Our proof relies on the contrast between kinks in the source distribution and the smoothness of real analytic functions. Real analytic functions comprise a broad class of generating mechanisms, and can be approximated with Normalizing Flows or Variational Autoencoders with standard activation functions (e.g., tanh, softplus, GELU), so our result applies with minimal changes to existing training pipelines. We perform experiments on real and synthetic data with both Normalizing Flows and Variational Auto-Encoders demonstrating their identifiability properties. In experiments on CelebA data we recover several interpretable latent factors controlling unique attributes across the dataset.

</details>


### [215] [Joint Remaining Useful Life Prediction and Capacity Estimation of Lithium-Ion Batteries Using Partial-Charging Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21932)
*Khoa Tran,Ho-Si-Hung Nguyen,Phone Wai Yan Moe,Hung-Cuong Trinh,Thi-Hoang-Giang Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一种"跨专家"框架，在仅使用部分充电测量、不依赖历史满循环容量数据的条件下，联合预测电池剩余使用寿命（RUL）与容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RUL预测与容量估计需要同时表征电池的长期渐变退化与近期行为，而实际中往往缺少历史满循环容量测量，如何在这种受限输入下实现两任务的联合准确预测是一个难点。

Method: 框架包含两个专家：RUL Expert以30循环历史中采样的10个循环的10分钟片段为输入，采用预训练GRU编码器、2D-CNN与时间GRU编码标称片段；Capacity Expert以10个连续循环的40分钟片段的统计描述符为输入，采用2D-CNN与Transformer。通过特征级线性调制（FiLM）模块，用短期表征对长期表征进行条件调制以实现联合预测。训练流程包括监督式自编码器预训练、各专家独立预训练，以及冻结专家后的融合训练。

Result: 在两个公开电池老化数据集上，参考配置的平均RUL RMSE分别为143.69与161.10循环，容量误差分别为12.36与7.28mAh；在Dataset I上融合使两项平均误差均优于任一单独专家。结果显示RUL与容量精度之间存在权衡：所提方法在两个数据集上取得对比方法中最低的RUL RMSE，但若干基线在容量误差上更优。

Conclusion: 跨专家融合框架可在无历史满循环容量测量条件下实现联合RUL与容量预测，并取得领先的RUL精度；但RUL与容量精度存在此消彼长的权衡关系，容量精度仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Joint remaining useful life (RUL) prediction and capacity estimation require representations of both gradual degradation and recent battery behavior. This paper presents a cross-expert framework using partial-charging measurements without measured historical full-cycle capacity as an input. The RUL Expert encodes nominal 10-min segments from ten cycles sampled within a 30-cycle history using a pretrained gated recurrent unit (GRU) encoder, a two-dimensional convolutional neural network (2D-CNN), and a temporal GRU. The Capacity Expert processes statistical descriptors of nominal 40-min segments from ten consecutive cycles using a 2D-CNN and a Transformer. A feature-wise linear modulation module uses the short-term representation to condition the long-term representation for joint prediction. Training comprises supervised autoencoder pretraining, independent expert pretraining, and fusion training with frozen experts. On two public battery-aging datasets, the reference configuration achieves mean RUL root-mean-square errors of 143.69 and 161.10 cycles and capacity errors of 12.36 and 7.28mAh, respectively. On Dataset I, fusion reduces both mean errors relative to either standalone expert. The results demonstrate a trade-off between RUL and capacity accuracy: the proposed method attains the lowest reported RUL RMSE among the compared methods on both datasets, whereas several baselines yield lower capacity errors.

</details>


### [216] [Learning to Move Cities: Deep Meta-Models and Reinforcement Policies for Calibration and Control in Urban Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21945)
*Adewumi Augustine Adepitan,Christopher J. Haruna,Oluwasegun Adegoke,Ayooluwatomiwa Ajiboye,Oluwatobi Oluwasakin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出共享潜空间框架，用同一学习表征连接仿真器标定与强化学习控制，显著提升标定样本效率并在基准网络中将系统总行程时间最多降低51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通网络优化面临两类难题：高保真仿真器的标定与实时运行控制。传统方法在大规模网络上存在计算瓶颈，且标定与控制通常被割裂处理，缺乏统一表征。

Method: 1）组合式MLP-自编码器架构，学习连接仿真器输入（OD需求、网络参数）与输出（行程时间、拥堵模式）的低维流形，用于高效贝叶斯优化标定；2）带经验回放与目标网络的深度Q学习智能体，通过调度与路径调整优化动态交通分配；3）将学习到的潜表征同时用于标定降维与强化学习状态表示。

Result: 相比传统降维方法具有更优的样本效率，在固定计算预算下对观测数据拟合更好；基准网络实验中系统总行程时间相比基线运营最多降低51%。

Conclusion: 共享潜空间范式为智能交通系统提供了从仿真器标定到自适应运行控制的统一路径，展示了深度学习在城市出行规划与管理中的潜力，尤其适用于传统优化方法受限的大规模网络。

Abstract: Urban transportation networks present complex optimization challenges spanning calibration of high-fidelity simulators and real-time operational control. This paper presents a shared latent-space framework that connects simulator calibration and reinforcement learning control through a common learned representation of urban traffic dynamics. First, we develop a combinatorial MLP-autoencoder architecture that learns low-dimensional manifolds linking simulator inputs (origin-destination demand, network parameters) to outputs (travel times, congestion patterns), enabling efficient Bayesian optimization for calibration. This approach demonstrates superior sample efficiency compared to traditional dimension reduction methods, achieving better fit to observational data within fixed computational budgets. Second, we implement a deep Q-learning agent with experience replay and target networks to optimize dynamic traffic assignment through scheduling and routing adjustments. In empirical evaluations on benchmark networks, our approach reduces system-wide travel times by up to 51% compared to baseline operations. The learned latent representation is not only used to reduce the dimensionality of Bayesian calibration, but is also incorporated into the reinforcement learning state representation, allowing the control policy to operate on compressed and calibrated traffic dynamics. This shared latent-space formulation provides a unified pathway from simulator calibration to adaptive operational control within intelligent transportation systems. Our results highlight the transformative potential of deep learning methods in urban mobility planning and management, particularly for large-scale networks where traditional optimization approaches face computational bottlenecks.

</details>


### [217] [RACER: Role-Aligned Competence Estimation for Human-AI Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21953)
*Joshua Strong,Emma Sun,Alexander Capstick,Pramit Saha,Cheng Ouyang,J. Alison Noble*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RACER——一种角色对齐的能力估计路由框架，用于在无需专家身份的情况下，通过上下文估计未见专家的能力并决定是否将预测任务转交给专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有pop-adaptive deferral方法（如L2D-Pop）可能出现与绝对类别坐标绑定的路由捷径，而IFD虽消除身份捷径，但其能力估计在每个类别内恒定，无法捕捉实例级专家专长。因此需要一种既能避免身份/绝对类别捷径、又能进行实例级能力估计的方法。

Method: RACER采用角色相对框架：在每个候选类别角色下估计专家对查询正确的后验预测概率，再与模型后验结合得到贝叶斯相关的专家正确概率。采用非参数与神经核池化估计器，基于候选角色关系、共享聚合和对称摘要，排除绝对类别身份通道。理论上证明类别重标记不变性，推导贝叶斯对齐的转交代理损失，并给出将路由遗憾与分类器及能力估计误差联系起来的插件式遗憾界。

Result: 在受控合成基准上，包括带有模拟专家的PathMNIST组织病理学上下文扩展研究，RACER在隐藏子类型依赖下能从额外上下文中获益；在CIFAR-100合成实验的独立采样未见专家划分上取得最强总体性能。在VinDr-CXR和CheXpert胸部X线基准上，RACER族在预算扫描转交设置中具有竞争力或最佳表现，但校准结果随指标和数据集而异。

Conclusion: RACER通过角色相对、身份无关的能力估计，实现了类别重标记不变且理论上有遗憾保证的群自适应转交，在合成与真实医学影像基准上总体表现优异，但校准表现尚不稳定。

Abstract: Learning to defer asks a predictive system when to act autonomously and when to defer to a human expert. Population-adaptive deferral extends this problem to unseen experts using a small context set of expert behavior. Neural context encoders such as L2D-Pop can be query-dependent, but may learn routing shortcuts tied to absolute class coordinates. Identity-Free Deferral (IFD) removes such shortcuts through role-indexed classwise competence profiles, but its estimates are constant within each class and cannot capture instance-level expert specialization. We propose RACER---Role-Aligned Competence Estimation for Routing---a role-relative framework for estimating an unseen expert's competence from context. RACER estimates the posterior-predictive probability that the expert is correct on a query under each candidate class role, then combines these estimates with the model posterior to obtain the Bayes-relevant expert-correctness probability. Nonparametric and neural kernel-pooling estimators use candidate-role relations, shared aggregation, and symmetric summaries, excluding absolute class-identity channels. We prove coherent class-relabelling invariance, derive a Bayes-aligned deferral surrogate, and give a plug-in regret bound relating routing regret to classifier and competence-estimation error. On controlled synthetic benchmarks, including a PathMNIST histopathology context-scaling study with simulated experts, RACER benefits from additional context under hidden subtype dependence and gives the strongest aggregate performance on a separately sampled unseen-expert split in the CIFAR-100 synthetic experiments. On the radiologist and human--AI chest-radiography benchmarks (VinDr-CXR and CheXpert), the RACER family is competitive or best in budget-swept deferral, with calibration results varying across metrics and datasets.

</details>


### [218] [Time series generation with spectrally aligned latent flow matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21989)
*Camilo Carvajal Reyes,Felipe Tobar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出谱对齐的隐空间流匹配时间序列生成器，通过傅里叶、小波与签名变换的微调损失，使合成样本在谱与局部结构上与真实数据对齐，兼顾真实性与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐空间流模型是时间序列生成中可靠且低成本的方法，但隐压缩会引入不希望的人为伪影（如与原始数据集的谱不匹配），从而限制其作为训练代理数据的可用性。核心动机是在隐空间训练中保留对合成样本适用性至关重要的动态特性。

Method: 构建谱对齐的隐空间流（latent-flow）时间序列生成器：在隐空间进行流匹配训练，并引入基于典型信号表示（Fourier、小波、签名变换）的微调损失。利用这些变换的可解释性，使合成信号在平滑度、目标谱内容等关键特征上与真实信号对齐，而非仅依赖逐点重建损失。在真实长程单变量与多变量基准数据集上，将所提对齐模型与基础隐流模型及当前最优方法进行对比。

Result: 定量结果表明，所提方法在反映信号真实性的指标上性能更优，计算效率更高，同时在局部结构上与训练集保持对齐，全面优于基础隐流模型与现有最优方法。

Conclusion: 通过在隐空间注入基于Fourier、小波与签名变换的谱对齐微调目标，可有效缓解隐压缩伪影，提升隐流生成时间序列的真实性与谱一致性，为构建高质量训练代理数据提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Latent flow models have proven to be a reliable and cost-effective method for time series generation. However, the latent compression induces unwanted artefacts, such as a spectral mismatch with respect to the underlying dataset, thus hindering their use as training surrogates. In this article, we propose a spectrally-aligned latent-flow time series generator, where the latent space for flow matching is trained to preserve dynamical properties that are relevant for the suitability of synthetic samples. We find that incorporating fine-tuning losses based on canonical signal representations such as the Fourier, wavelet and signature transforms helps overcome these issues. The interpretability of these transformations allows us to ensure that the synthetic signals are aligned with the true ones in terms of relevant features, such as smoothness or targeted spectral content, as opposed to relying on pointwise reconstruction losses only. We compare the proposed aligned models against a base latent-flow model and the state of the art over real-world long-range univariate and multivariate benchmark datasets. Our quantitative results validate the superiority of the proposed method in terms of its performance on metrics reflecting signal realness and computational efficiency, while being aligned to the training set with respect to its local structure.

</details>


### [219] [Assessment of Machine Learning-Based Critical Heat Flux Models in the CTF Subchannel Code for Square Rod Bundle Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21995)
*Aidan Furlong,Vinicius de Melo Monteiro,Robert Salko,Juliana Pacheco Duarte,Xu Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在CTF子通道程序中基于EPRI棒束CHF数据库评估了管束训练得到的机器学习CHF模型，发现其可较好地迁移至方形棒束工况，总体优于Bowring、W-3关联式与2006 Groeneveld LUT，其中局部混合LUT模型表现最佳、半局部纯ML模型亦具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界热流密度（CHF）直接关系燃料性能与反应堆安全，是核热工水力学中的关键安全参量。相比传统经验关联式与查找表（LUT），机器学习方法能显著提高CHF预测精度，但现有ML-CHF模型多基于圆管数据库开发与评估，其在反应堆相关的棒束几何中的适用性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 将基于机器学习（ML）的CHF模型集成于CTF子通道程序，利用美国电力研究院（EPRI）棒束CHF数据库进行评估；模型涵盖纯ML模型与混合残差修正模型，并分别采用局部（local）与半局部（semilocal）两种建模形式。

Result: 管束训练的ML-CHF模型能较好地迁移至棒束应用，在多数几何与运行工况下优于传统CHF方法。局部混合LUT模型整体性能最强，半局部纯ML模型亦极具竞争力。与Bowring关联式、W-3关联式及2006 Groeneveld LUT的对比表明，即使仅用圆管数据训练，也能在棒束CHF预测上取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 本研究首次在工业级子通道分析环境中对ML-CHF模型在方形棒束内开展了大规模评估，验证了其跨几何迁移能力，支持其在反应堆热工水力分析中的推广应用。

Abstract: The prediction of critical heat flux (CHF), a key safety-related quantity in nuclear thermal hydraulics, remains an important challenge due to its direct relationship with fuel performance and reactor safety. Recent studies have demonstrated that relative to traditional empirical correlations and lookup tables (LUTs), machine learning (ML) methods can substantially improve CHF prediction accuracy. Most ML-based CHF models, however, have been developed and evaluated using tube databases, leaving their applicability to reactor-relevant rod bundle geometries largely unexplored.
  This study evaluates ML-based CHF models deployed within the CTF subchannel code using the Electric Power Research Institute (EPRI) rod bundle CHF database. Both pure and hybrid residual correction models are considered in local and semilocal formulations. The tube-trained ML CHF models generally transferred favorably to rod bundle applications and outperformed traditional CHF methods across most geometries and operating conditions. The local hybrid LUT model produced the strongest overall performance, and the semilocal pure ML model remained highly competitive. Comparison against the Bowring correlation, W-3 correlation, and 2006 Groeneveld LUT demonstrated that substantial improvements in rod bundle CHF prediction are possible even when models are trained exclusively on tube data. These findings provide one of the first large-scale assessments of ML-based CHF models in square rod bundles within a production-level subchannel analysis environment and support their broader application in reactor thermal hydraulic analysis.

</details>


### [220] [Abstention and Noise Filtering: Two Missing Primitives of Softmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22005)
*Richard Zhe Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文论证注意力门控提供"弃权"与"噪声过滤"两种能力，并通过10M–350M参数实验揭示二者随规模此消彼长，双机制并用最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究报告门控注意力价值通路可改进语言模型预训练，但对其原因存在分歧。作者主张门控提供了softmax注意力所缺失的两种能力：弃权与噪声过滤，并试图用实验厘清各自贡献。

Method: 在10M至350M参数的匹配模型中，通过可学习的逐头sink logit在softmax中实现弃权，通过在每个value上加门控实现噪声过滤；并向注意力头读取的value注入受控干扰以验证门控作用。

Result: 弃权带来的验证损失下降随模型增大而减弱，噪声过滤的收益则随规模上升；10M时弃权贡献几乎全部增益，350M时过滤贡献大部分；各规模最佳模型均同时包含两种原语；注入干扰实验证实门控可消除干扰，并揭示两种门控形式各自的盲区。

Conclusion: 门控的收益来自弃权与噪声过滤两种互补机制，二者随规模各有优势；同时提供两种原语仅增加可忽略的参数，且与键值缓存兼容，是各规模下的最优方案。

Abstract: Gating the value pathway of attention reportedly improves language model pretraining, and prior studies disagree on why. We argue and provide experimental evidence that such gates supply two different things that softmax attention lacks: abstention and noise filtering. The first is abstention, which allows an attention head to output nothing, bypassing the requirement that attention weights must sum to one. The second is noise filtering, which allows the value pathway of an attention head to suppress interference from superposed features in the residual stream. In our experiments in matched models from 10M to 350M parameters, we supply abstention through a learned per-head sink logit in the softmax and noise filtering through a gate on each value. We report three empirical findings. First, the benefit of abstention, measured as the reduction in validation loss relative to a matched baseline, declines as models grow, whereas the benefit of noise filtering increases with scale. In particular, abstention accounts for nearly all of the gain from gating at 10M and filtering for most of it at 350M. Second, the best model at every scale is the one with both primitives built in. Third, injecting controlled interference into the values a head reads confirms that the gate removes such interference, and reveals that each of the two gate forms we study has a characteristic blind spot. Supplying both primitives adds negligible parameters and remains compatible with the key-value cache.

</details>


### [221] [COMPLEX: A Closed-Form Certified Embedding of Multiparameter Persistence Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22012)
*Sushovan Majhi,Atish Mitra,Žiga Virk,Pramita Bagchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出COMPLEX，一种无需训练、闭式的多参数持久性模块嵌入（vectorization）方法，并首次为该类特征映射给出双侧失真界（two-sided distortion bound），使"忠实性"可被度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多参数持久性vectorization只有单侧Lipschitz上界，缺乏下界（lower gauge），因而无法说明特征是否忠实，也无法建立逐预测（per-prediction）的保证。本文旨在补齐缺失的下界一侧。

Method: COMPLEX将模块沿固定的近对角网格切片，用经过认证的PLACE/PALACE landmark映射嵌入每个切片barcode，再拼接。在可检验的"见证切片一致性"（witnessing-slice coherence）条件下，单个切片即可提供闭式下界。此外采用闭式选择landmark半径、保证书核函数与双滤流（bifiltration）集合。

Result: 在Orbit5k全部审计样本对上一致性条件成立。下界紧致（与实现距离相差很小因子），但操作上是局部的：RBF-SVM达91%，而1-NN仅78%，说明逐预测认证因landmark嵌入的结构性原因失效。仅用交叉验证的SVM头（无学习嵌入、无留出校准），COMPLEX在Orbit5k达91.95%，Orbit100k达92.98%，达到SOTA。在图数据上于四个共享分子基准上均超过GRIL，且是唯一在COX2上超多数类基线3分以上的多参数方法。

Conclusion: COMPLEX给出首个多参数特征映射的双侧失真界，使忠实性可度量；闭式选择组件可进一步提升精度，而梯度式自适应无增益；方法在Orbit与图基准上达到或超越当前最优。

Abstract: Every multiparameter persistence vectorization we know of carries a one-sided Lipschitz upper bound and nothing below it: without a lower gauge there is no sense in which the features are faithful, and no per-prediction guarantee can be built on them. This paper supplies the missing side. COMPLEX is a closed-form, training-free embedding of multiparameter modules -- slice the module along a fixed near-diagonal net, embed each slice barcode by the certified PLACE/PALACE landmark map, concatenate. Under a checkable witnessing-slice coherence condition, holding on 100% of audited pairs on Orbit5k, a single slice carries a closed-form lower gauge: separated modules stay separated in the embedding. With the standard upper bound this gives, to our knowledge, the first two-sided distortion bound for a multiparameter feature map, making faithfulness measurable. Measuring it, we find the floor tight within a small factor of realized distances yet operationally local: an RBF-SVM reaches 91% where 1-NN reaches 78% on the same features. Local per-prediction certification therefore fails for a structural reason common to every landmark embedding whose lower gauge is witnessed by one coordinate. With no learned embedding and no held-out calibration -- only a cross-validated SVM head -- COMPLEX sets the state of the art on both Orbit benchmarks (91.95% on Orbit5k, 92.98% on Orbit100k), level with or above Euler-characteristic surfaces and above transformers and graphcode. On graphs it exceeds GRIL on all four shared molecular benchmarks with one fixed configuration, including the only multiparameter method to clear COX2's majority baseline by more than three points. Closed-form selection -- of the landmark radius, the kernel (certificate-preserving), and the bifiltration set -- buys further accuracy; gradient-shaped adaptation buys none.

</details>


### [222] [$λ$-Controlled GRPO: Turning Flow-Matching Ratio Instability into a Budgeted Resource](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22041)
*Yufeng Wang,Parivesh Priye,Meeshawn Marathe,Ramit Pahwa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 λ-Controlled GRPO，指出流匹配模型强化学习训练中的多种不稳定现象（重要性比漂移、离散、裁剪率差异等）源自同一个可精确估计的逐步骤量——路径方差，并据此从预测规律而非噪声经验统计来校准重要性比、按预测代价分配各去噪步的梯度。在文生图任务上，该方法在文本准确率与人类偏好奖励上均超越最强经验稳定器，并将后期步路径方差控制在预算内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow-GRPO 将去噪采样器视为可优化的随机策略，但多步去噪场景下训练不稳定：策略更新随去噪步系统性变化，重要性比持续低于 1、离散度增大、裁剪率不一致，后期可用样本减少。已有工作把这些现象当作彼此独立的失效模式，各自用人工调参的稳定器修补，缺乏统一解释与机理性的处理方式。

Method: 作者证明上述现象均源于单一的逐步骤量——路径方差（path variance）。该量由采样器的高斯转移核精确决定，并可在训练中廉价估计。由此把不稳定性重新定义为可度量、可预算的“资源”。方法 λ-Controlled GRPO 依据该预测规律（而非噪声经验统计）校准重要性比行为，并按各去噪步的预测代价分配梯度努力；控制更新的两个尺度由标准策略选择确定，而非引入为自由调参参数。

Result: 在文生图模型、两种奖励设定下（用 OCR 评分的困难目标文本渲染，以及用偏好模型评分的人类偏好对齐），λ-Controlled GRPO 在文本准确率和偏好奖励上均优于最强经验稳定器；并且在基线系统性超出的后期步上，将路径方差保持在预期预算之内。

Conclusion: 该工作把 Flow-GRPO 的不稳定性从“事后修修补补的症状集合”重构为可由采样器转移律精确刻画并预算的单一机制。所得到的更新由其自身转移律校准，而非在失稳出现后被动稳定化，为多步去噪强化学习提供了统一、机理驱动的稳定化框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is increasingly used to align image generators with reward signals, and Flow-GRPO recently extended this paradigm to flow-matching models by treating the denoising sampler as a stochastic policy that can be optimized from reward feedback. Training in this setting is unstable in a way specific to multi-step denoising: the policy update changes systematically across denoising steps, with importance ratios drifting below one, becoming increasingly dispersed, clipping at different rates, and leaving fewer usable samples late in training. Prior work treats these effects as separate failure modes and addresses each with a hand-tuned stabilizer. We show instead that they arise from a single per-step quantity, which we call path variance. This quantity is determined exactly by the sampler's Gaussian transition kernel and can be estimated cheaply during training. This reframes instability as a resource that can be measured and budgeted rather than a collection of symptoms to repair. Our method, $λ$-Controlled GRPO, calibrates importance-ratio behavior from this predicted law rather than from noisy empirical statistics, and allocates gradient effort across denoising steps according to their predicted cost. The two scales governing the update are fixed by standard policy choices rather than introduced as free tuning parameters. On a text-to-image model under two reward settings, rendering difficult target text scored by optical character recognition and matching human preferences scored by a preference model, $λ$-Controlled GRPO improves both text accuracy and preference reward over the strongest empirical stabilizer. It also keeps late-step path variance within its intended budget, precisely where the baseline systematically overshoots. The result is a Flow-GRPO update calibrated by its own transition law rather than stabilized after instability appears.

</details>


### [223] [Available Guardrails: Certifying Selective Prediction across ML Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22048)
*Parivesh Priye,Yufeng Wang,Haibin Ling,Michael Chaykowsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究选择性预测器（safety gate）在有限校准数据下能否对每个报告单元（工具、策略标签、患者亚组等）在目标精度上获得认证的问题，提出"可用性（availability）"的可计算定义，并将报告分区选择形式化为动态规划问题，揭示安全性、粒度与服务流量之间的权衡前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署场景要求选择性预测器对每个报告单元都能在目标精度上获得认证，但难点往往不在于已颁发的认证是否有效，而在于有限的校准数据是否足以产生认证。当门控更安全或粒度更细时，某些单元会因证据不足而无法认证，因此需要一种可计算的"可用性"概念来刻画这一现象。

Method: 基于经典精确二项式反演（exact-binomial inversion）定义可计算的可用性；在固定组序下，将报告分区选择形式化为动态规划，以刻画安全性、粒度与服务流量之间的权衡；采用在一个规划划分（planning split）上构造候选分区、在另一个划分上选择的方法；并提出在报告单元间重新分配族错误率预算（familywise error budget）这一保持有效性的调节手段。

Result: 前沿分析显示存在被有限样本估计几乎抹去的大量总体机会：真值知情规划器相比 support balancing 获得 0.157 的平均覆盖提升，而朴素估计器仅恢复 0.005。跨划分的候选构造与选择可恢复部分差距，平均覆盖较 support balancing 提升 0.060，且该方向在三个意图路由数据集、两种架构下的 60 个模型效应中复现于 59 个。重新分配族错误率预算在总体量与含噪估计下均能进一步提升覆盖。该前沿在 LLM 工具调用、内容审核、病变分类与推荐任务中均出现，且带有预测器特定的上限。

Conclusion: 认证可用性是一种可规划的部署资源，它决定了安全门控能否被认证、以何种粒度认证，以及覆盖多少流量。

Abstract: A selective predictor acts as a safety gate: it returns an output only when the prediction appears sufficiently trustworthy. Deployments increasingly require this reliability to be certified at a target precision for every reporting unit of interest, such as a tool, policy label, or patient subgroup. The main difficulty is often not whether a granted certificate is valid, but whether finite calibration data can produce one at all. As the gate becomes safer or more fine-grained, some units may receive too little evidence to certify. We make this notion of availability computable through classical exact-binomial inversion and formulate reporting-partition selection, under a fixed group order, as a dynamic program that exposes the trade-off among safety, granularity, and served traffic. The resulting frontier reveals a large population opportunity that finite-sample estimation nearly erases: a truth-informed planner gains $0.157$ mean coverage over support balancing, whereas a naive estimator recovers only $0.005$, making recovery from finite data the central challenge. Constructing candidate partitions on one planning split and selecting among them on another recovers part of this gap, improving mean coverage over support balancing by $0.060$, with the direction reproduced in $59$ of $60$ model effects across three intent-routing datasets and two architectures. A complementary validity-preserving lever, reallocating the familywise error budget across reporting units, recovers additional coverage both with population quantities and noisy estimates. The same frontier recurs, with predictor-specific ceilings, across LLM tool-calling, content moderation, lesion classification, and recommendation. Certified availability is therefore a plannable deployment resource that determines when a safety gate can be certified, at what granularity, and over how much traffic.

</details>


### [224] [Particle Competition and Cooperation for Robust Graph Convolutional Network Learning Under Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22053)
*Fabricio Breve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PCC+GCN混合框架，用粒子竞争与合作(PCC)在GCN训练前进行图标签精炼，在多种标签噪声下取得最高的平均精度与平均排名，且计算开销显著更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GCN对标签噪声高度敏感，错误的监督信号会沿图结构传播并破坏节点表示学习。现有鲁棒方法或精度受限或计算代价高，需要一种既能有效识别并修正可疑标签、又计算高效的预处理策略。

Method: 将PCC作为GCN训练前的图标签精炼阶段：利用粒子支配(domination)动力学识别可疑标注节点，判定其标签应保留、剔除或重新分配；PCC使用的图可加入基于特征的k近邻边增强，而GCN仍在原始图结构与节点特征上训练。在NoisyGL基准的10个图数据集上评估Uniform、Pair、Random及实例依赖(instance-dependent)噪声，并在Cora、CiteSeer、PubMed上进行详细超参数分析。

Result: 常规噪声下PCC+GCN取得最高总体平均精度与最佳平均排名，在干净及全部噪声场景下相对基线GCN平均提升1.67个百分点；实例依赖噪声下与最优鲁棒方法竞争力相当，同时执行时间大幅降低，在10个数据集中有8个为最快的鲁棒方法。

Conclusion: 基于PCC的标签精炼是一种有效且计算高效的预处理策略，可在噪声监督下显著提升GCN的鲁棒性，在精度与效率之间取得良好折中。

Abstract: Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs) are highly sensitive to label noise, since corrupted supervision can propagate through the graph and degrade learned node representations. This work proposes PCC+GCN, a hybrid framework that uses Particle Competition and Cooperation (PCC) as a graph-based label-refinement stage before GCN training. PCC identifies suspicious labeled nodes through particle domination dynamics and determines whether their labels should be preserved, removed, or reassigned before GCN training. The framework also allows the graph used by PCC to be augmented with feature-based $k$-nearest-neighbor edges, while the GCN itself is trained on the original graph structure and node features. The proposed method was evaluated on ten graph datasets from the NoisyGL benchmark under conventional Uniform, Pair, and Random label noise, as well as under instance-dependent label noise. A detailed hyperparameter analysis was also conducted on Cora, CiteSeer, and PubMed. Under conventional noise, PCC+GCN achieved the highest overall average accuracy and the best average rank among the evaluated methods, with an average gain of $1.67$ percentage points over the baseline GCN across the clean setting and all noisy scenarios. Under instance-dependent noise, PCC+GCN remained competitive with the best-performing robust methods while requiring substantially lower execution time, being the fastest robust method on eight of the ten datasets. The results indicate that PCC-based label refinement provides an effective and computationally efficient preprocessing strategy for improving GCN robustness under noisy supervision.

</details>


### [225] [Benchmarking World Models for Continual Learning on Compositional Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22055)
*Haoyu Zhou,Joe Watson,Anson Lei,Ingmar Posner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出面向机器人操作世界模型的组合式持续学习基准，通过将任务课程设计为已见任务的组合，并将组合按动作与感知两个轴分解，从而把"知识复用"从"适应能力"中隔离出来进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型理想上应具备跨任务持续学习、适应新环境且不遗忘旧知识的能力，其中对先前经验知识的保留与复用是高效适应的关键，因为物理世界动力学常由重复出现的机制描述。然而现有世界模型的适应度指标混淆了两种能力：学习未见任务的速度与容量，以及对已获得知识的复用——因为新任务总是同时包含新颖内容与重复内容。作者希望将知识复用从先前经验中单独剥离出来加以衡量。

Method: 作者提出一个面向机器人操作世界模型的组合式持续学习基准。具体而言，为每个任务课程设计组合任务，即由序列中已出现任务的部分特征组合而成；并进一步沿动作（action）与感知（perception）两个轴对组合进行分解，以理解不同输入模态如何成为知识复用的瓶颈。在评估上，作者在经典持续学习方法下评测当前最先进的世界模型，并对比一个模块化世界模型——其动力学主干包含显式可复用的组件。

Result: 实验结果显示，模块化设计在权衡"复用"与"遗忘"方面优于常规方法，即更好地平衡了知识复用与灾难性遗忘；但没有一个方法能完全解决该问题，说明构建"可复用且不遗忘"的持续世界模型仍有明显研究空间。

Conclusion: 该工作通过组合式基准将知识复用与任务适应能力解耦，揭示了现有世界模型在持续学习中的局限：模块化有助于缓解复用与遗忘的权衡，但尚不足以彻底解决。作者由此指出，专门面向"无遗忘复用"的持续世界模型是一个有待开拓的方向。

Abstract: A desirable property of a world model is the ability to learn continually across tasks, adapting to new environments without forgetting what the agent has already learnt. In particular, the ability to retain and reuse knowledge obtained from prior experiences underpins an agent's ability to efficiently adapt to novel environments, as the dynamics of the physical world can often be described in recurring mechanisms. However, the world model's measure of adaptation entangles two abilities: the speed and capacity to learn unseen tasks, and the reuse of knowledge already acquired, since incoming tasks carry novel content alongside what recurs. In order to isolate knowledge reuse from prior experiences, we propose a compositional continual learning benchmark for world models in robot manipulation. Specifically, we design each task curriculum with compositional tasks that combine aspects of the tasks seen in the sequence. We further factorise this composition along the axes of action and perception to better understand how different input modalities bottleneck knowledge reuse. We evaluate state-of-the-art world models under canonical continual learning methods, alongside a modular world model whose dynamics backbone contains explicitly reusable components. Results show that modularity balances reuse against forgetting better than conventional methods, but none solve the problem fully, leaving clear room for continual world models built to reuse without forgetting. More details are available on our project website: https://object814.github.io/Compositional-Continual-Learning/.

</details>


### [226] [BrainWideBench: Benchmarking large-scale pretraining and across-animal transfer in multi-region neural recordings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22064)
*Alexandre Andre,Shivashriganesh P. Mahato,Vinam Arora,Keshav Balaji,Divyansha Lachi,Nanda H. Krishna,Jingyun Xiao,Yizi Zhang,Ximeng Mao,Wenrui Ma,Han Yu,International Brain Laboratory,Daniel Birman,Niccolò Bonacchi,Gaelle A. Chapuis,Joana A. Catarino,Felicia Davatolhagh,Mayo Faulkner,Laura Freitas-Silva,Fei Hu,Julia M. Huntenburg,Anup Khanal,Inês Laranjeira,Petrina Lau,Guido T. Meijer,Nathaniel J. Miska,Jean-Paul Noel,Alejandro Pan-Vazquez,Georg Raiser,Cyrille Rossant,Karolina Z. Socha,Anne E. Urai,Miles J. Wells,Steven J. West,Olivier Winter,Blake Richards,Guillaume Lajoie,Cole Hurwitz,Mehdi Azabou,Matthew R. Whiteway,Liam Paninski,Eva L. Dyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出BrainWideBench，一个基于IBL Brainwide Map（139只小鼠、276个脑区）评测跨个体神经表征迁移能力的基准，含行为解码、神经活动预测和脑区解剖组织恢复三套任务，揭示了预训练方法的迁移能力高度依赖预训练目标与下游任务的匹配度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模神经记录技术已能跨多动物、多脑区采集数据，理论上可借此学习通用的神经表征；但现有研究受限于碎片化的评测协议和对单一任务域的窄化关注，缺乏统一可复现的评测框架，因此难以衡量朝向通用脑模型的进展。

Method: 构建BrainWideBench基准，数据源自International Brain Laboratory Brainwide Map数据集，包含139只执行感觉引导决策任务的小鼠、覆盖276个脑区的神经与行为记录。基准围绕三个互补任务套件设计：（1）下游行为解码；（2）掩码或未来神经活动预测；（3）恢复具有生物学意义的解剖组织。在此之上系统评测多种预训练方法，涵盖下游微调与对未见动物的零样本泛化两类迁移设置，并与单会话匹配基线对比。

Result: 预训练相比匹配的单会话基线能提升性能；但现有方法在迁移能力上表现出异质性——增益强烈依赖预训练目标与下游任务的一致性；没有任何单一方法能在三套任务上均表现优异，且多数方法仅针对其中一部分任务设计。

Conclusion: 学习能够同时在行为、神经动力学和解剖结构上泛化的表征仍是未解难题；BrainWideBench通过提供统一、可复现的评测套件，为衡量朝向小鼠大脑通用模型的进展建立了框架。

Abstract: Advances in large-scale neural recording have made it possible to collect data across many animals and distributed brain regions, raising the question of whether this scale can be exploited to learn general-purpose neural representations transferable across diverse downstream tasks. Yet, progress toward this goal has been limited by fragmented evaluation protocols and a narrow focus on individual task domains. Here, we present BrainWideBench, a benchmark for evaluating across-animal transfer on multi-region neural recordings, built on the International Brain Laboratory Brainwide Map dataset of neural and behavioral recordings spanning 276 brain regions from 139 mice performing a sensory-guided decision-making task. The benchmark is organized around three complementary task suites that evaluate whether learned representations support downstream decoding of behavior, can predict masked or future neural activity, and can recover biologically meaningful anatomical organization. With this benchmark, we systematically evaluate pretraining methods across transfer settings, including finetuning on downstream objectives and zero-shot generalization to unseen animals. Our results confirm pretraining improves performance over matched single-session baselines, but we show current methods exhibit heterogeneity in transfer capabilities: gains depend strongly on the alignment between pretraining objectives and downstream tasks. No single approach performs uniformly well across all three suites, and most methods are designed to only address a subset of them. Together, these findings suggest that learning representations that jointly generalize across behavior, dynamics, and anatomy remains an open challenge. By providing a unified and reproducible evaluation suite, BrainWideBench establishes a framework for measuring progress toward general-purpose models of the mouse brain.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [RBS-Attention: Radius-Bounded Sparse Prefill for Long-Context Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20971)
*Chuxu Song,Jiuqi Wei,Zhencan Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练稀疏预填充方法RBS-Attention，用双分支（质心基分支+救援分支）选择机制解决"均值稀释"问题，在H100上实现最高20.65倍预填充注意力加速，同时保持接近稠密注意力的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理受限于预填充阶段，因为稠密自注意力需在生成前处理整个提示。稀疏块选择可降低成本，但块质心（centroid）可能掩盖块内少数高相关token——作者称此失败模式为"均值稀释（mean dilution）"，导致真正重要的块被低估、漏选。

Method: RBS-Attention是免训练稀疏预填充方法，含两个互补选择分支：1）质心基分支捕捉平均相关性；2）救援分支利用最大key块半径及其与提示、层、头相关的分布，识别有被低估风险的块。两分支独立阈值化后合并mask，控制救援块贡献，同时保持常规块稀疏FlashAttention执行。

Result: 在H100 GPU、128K上下文、Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507-FP8上，实现20.65倍独立预填充注意力加速、11.92倍vLLM预填充注意力加速、5.97倍端到端首token时间（TTFT）加速。稠密Qwen3-32B上RULER总分88.65，对比稠密注意力的89.52；另用LongBench-v2、InfiniteBench、Video-MME评估质量。支持性实验测量实际保留率、在匹配密度下比较选择器，并刻画块大小、阈值与内存行为。

Conclusion: 结果表明半径自适应双分支选择是应对长上下文预填充的有效方法，能在显著加速的同时保持接近稠密注意力的质量。

Abstract: Long-context large language model inference is increasingly limited by prefill, where dense self-attention processes the entire prompt before generation begins. Sparse block selection can reduce this cost, but a block centroid may hide a highly relevant token among many irrelevant ones. We call this failure mode mean dilution and propose RBS-Attention, a training-free sparse-prefill method with two complementary selection branches. A centroid base branch captures average relevance, while a rescue branch uses the maximum key-block radius and its prompt-, layer-, and head-dependent distribution to identify blocks at risk of underestimation. Independently thresholding the two branches and combining their masks controls the contribution of rescue blocks while preserving regular block-sparse FlashAttention execution. On H100 GPUs, RBS-Attention achieves 20.65$\times$ standalone prefill-attention speedup, 11.92$\times$ vLLM prefill-attention speedup, and 5.97$\times$ end-to-end time-to-first-token speedup at 128K on Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507-FP8. On the dense Qwen3-32B model, it obtains 88.65 overall RULER accuracy versus 89.52 for dense attention; LongBench-v2, InfiniteBench, and Video-MME provide additional quality evaluation. Supporting experiments measure actual retention, compare selectors at matched density, and characterize block-size, threshold, and memory behavior. Together, these results support radius-adaptive dual-branch selection as an effective approach to long-context prefill.

</details>


### [228] [Attention-Aware Routing: Coupling Routing and Attention in MoEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20974)
*Despoina Kosmopoulou,Anastasios Tsetsilas,Efthymios Georgiou,Giannis Karamanolakis,Swastik Roy,Alexandros Potamianos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Attention-Aware Routing (AAR)，用滑动窗口注意力权重提取的时序与频谱特征增强MoE路由器，在冻结主干、仅训练路由参数下提升GSM8K +3.37pp，并揭示路由-注意力耦合回路、缓解发散生成及深度敏感的路由-推理张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE语言模型的router仅依据token的hidden state在有限上下文下选专家并赋权，缺乏对模型全局上下文状态的利用，路由信息基础薄弱。

Method: AAR用滑动窗口内attention weights提取的时序与频谱特征增强router，这些特征被视为模型上下文状态的摘要且与hidden state解耦；完全冻结基础transformer，仅训练路由参数，将路由作为唯一变量进行隔离研究。

Result: 在OLMoE上AAR较routing-only SFT基线在GSM8K提升+3.37pp；路由在第l层的变化经残差流传播放大第l+1层attention sink，在不更新注意力机制的情况下重塑注意力；AAR减少长发散生成（错误答案变短、正确答案长度不变）；深度敏感：全面施加会损害事实检索，深层引入则保留数学推理增益。

Conclusion: 路由与注意力构成耦合回路；AAR暴露了跨深度的检索-推理张力，使层选择性AAR成为探查不同层注意力所携带路由相关信息的受控探针。

Abstract: In Mixture-of-Experts language models, the router typically selects and weights experts based on the token's hidden state, utilizing limited contextual information. We propose Attention-Aware Routing (AAR), which augments the router with temporal and spectral features extracted from a sliding window of attention weights that represent a summary of the model's contextual state, disentangled from the hidden state. Keeping the base transformer entirely frozen, we train only the routing parameters, isolating routing as the sole variable. AAR improves GSM8K by +3.37 pp over a routing-only SFT baseline on OLMoE. Beyond performance, we show that routing and attention form a coupled circuit: routing changes at layer l propagate through the residual stream to amplify attention sinks at layer l+1, reshaping attention without any direct update to the attention mechanism itself. Further, AAR reduces long diverging generation, with incorrect answers getting shorter, while correct answers remain unchanged in length. Finally, AAR is strongly depth-sensitive: applying it indiscriminately across layers can degrade factual retrieval, whereas mathematical reasoning gains persist when it is introduced deeper in the network. This sensitivity exposes a retrieval--reasoning tension across depth and makes layer-selective AAR a controlled probe of the routing-relevant information carried by attention at different layers.

</details>


### [229] [CaLR: Causal Latent Revision for Robust Diffusion Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20981)
*Wei Cai,Jian Zhao,Yuchen Yuan,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Causal Latent Revision (CaLR)框架，将推理重构为受约束的隐空间优化，结合AR与扩散语言模型优势，实现并行生成中的动态自我纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型存在局部贪心问题，扩散语言模型缺乏推理所需的严格因果结构，两者各有缺陷，作者希望结合二者优势并克服不足。

Method: 采用来自专家模型的因果拓扑矩阵（CTM）与隐式微分，通过梯度引导的"思维修订"来强制逻辑一致性，从而在并行生成过程中实现中间步骤的动态自我纠错。

Result: 在复杂基准上取得扩散语言模型的SOTA表现，超越强自回归基线，并在数独等受约束任务中展现出更优的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CaLR通过约束隐优化统一了AR的因果结构与DLM的并行生成能力，验证了gradient-guided thought revision在推理任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) models suffer from local greediness, while diffusion language models (DLMs) often lack the strict causal structure required for reasoning. To combine the advantages and overcome the drawbacks of the dual, we propose Causal Latent Revision (CaLR), a framework that reformulates reasoning as constrained latent optimization. By adopting a causal topology matrix (CTM) from an expert model and implicit differentiation, CaLR performs gradient-guided ``thought revision" to enforce logical consistency, enabling dynamic self-correction of intermediate steps during parallel generation. Empirically, CaLR achieves SOTA DLM performance on complex benchmarks, surpassing strong AR baselines and demonstrating superior robustness in constrained tasks like Sudoku.

</details>


### [230] [LoRA Enhanced Contrastive Learning with SAS Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21061)
*Dan Zimmerman,Frank E. Bobe,Amelia L. McCormack,Matthew Cook,Gregory D. Vetaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将在海实测合成孔径声呐（SAS）自动目标识别中适配DINOv3 ViT，采用LoRA+难负样本挖掘+监督对比学习的三阶段参数高效框架。结果显示仅LoRA（Rank 4，训练0.26%权重）带来主要增益（AUPRC由0.300升至0.679±0.027），后两阶段相对匹配对照均为零效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAS ATR对海军能力具有支撑价值，但深度学习受限于目标图像稀缺、背景杂波强以及需人工介入评估；同时自然图像预训练特征与水下声传播特性之间存在域差距。

Method: 三阶段参数高效适配：阶段1冻结ViT主干仅用LoRA微调，以桥接自然图像预训练与水下声学域差距；阶段2用难负样本挖掘强化对岩石、沉积物等声学"模仿物"的决策边界；阶段3用监督对比学习（SupCon）分离目标与杂波表征。评估采用任务级地理划分的实测SAS数据，在85%测试召回率下比较各方案，并以三个随机种子重复。

Result: LoRA是主导因素：在相同冻结主干下将AUPRC从0.300提升至0.679±0.027，Rank 4即可实现且仅训练0.26%参数。两个精炼阶段均未超越匹配对照：难负样本挖掘相对等规模随机课程AUPRC变化-0.0045±0.0119；SupCon相对前一阶段变化+0.0002±0.0096。

Conclusion: 上述零结果表明，挖掘所针对的数据已被编码器拟合，且监督阶段已基本塑造出目标—杂波几何结构。因此一个高效的适配阶段即已足够，堆叠式精炼并无必要。

Abstract: Automatic target recognition (ATR) with synthetic aperture sonar (SAS) supports advanced naval capabilities, but deep learning is constrained by scarce target imagery, background clutter, and human-in-the-loop assessment. We adapt DINOv3 Vision Transformer (ViT) models to underwater SAS ATR using a three-stage parameter-efficient framework. Stage 1 uses Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) while freezing the ViT backbone, bridging the gap between natural-image pretraining and underwater acoustic propagation. Stage 2 uses hard-negative mining to strengthen the decision boundary against acoustic mimics, including rocks and sediment formations resembling man-made targets. Stage 3 uses Supervised Contrastive Learning (SupCon) to separate target and clutter representations. We evaluate at-sea SAS data using a mission-level geographic split, compare all arms at 85 percent test recall, and repeat each comparison over three random seeds. LoRA accounts for the primary effect, increasing area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) from 0.300 to 0.679 +/- 0.027 using the same frozen backbone. Rank 4 achieves this result while training only 0.26 percent of weights. Neither refinement stage exceeds its matched control: hard-negative mining changes AUPRC by -0.0045 +/- 0.0119 versus an equal-size random curriculum, and SupCon changes AUPRC by +0.0002 +/- 0.0096 versus the preceding stage. These null results indicate that mining occurred on data the encoder had already fit and that supervised stages had already imposed most target-clutter geometry. One efficient adaptation stage is sufficient; stacked refinement is not.

</details>


### [231] [Detecting Hallucination in LLMs: Tracing the Topological Signatures of Impaired Context Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21096)
*Amir Jalilifard,Anderson Rocha,Eric Wong,Marcos Medeiros Raimundo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从注意力图的拓扑视角研究信息流动模式，利用Forman-Ricci曲率识别注意力图中的信息瓶颈结构，提出一种同时捕捉半局部与全局信息流特征的单次前向方法，用于区分大模型幻觉与非幻觉回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力的幻觉检测方法难以有效刻画信息流动的结构性异常，作者希望借助拓扑几何工具揭示幻觉与注意力图信息瓶颈之间的关联。

Method: 分析注意力图的Forman-Ricci曲率以识别信息瓶颈；提取与幻觉相关的注意力头的半局部与全局信息流特征；采用单次前向（single-pass）检测框架。

Result: 在多个LLM和两个幻觉检测基准上，所提单次前向方法一致优于现有基于注意力与多响应（multi-response）的基线，并在不同LLM架构上保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 因果生成过程中token间上下文共享受损与幻觉发生强相关；幻觉回答持续表现为过度依赖自注意力、对早期token的上下文检索分散，以及信息过度挤压（information over-squashing），在最后一层Transformer中尤为明显。

Abstract: In this work, we examine the topology of information flow patterns within attention graphs to effectively distinguish hallucinated from non-hallucinated responses. We analyze the Forman-Ricci curvature to identify structural patterns indicating information bottlenecks in attention graphs. We then introduce a method that captures both semi-local and global information-flow characteristics of attention heads associated with hallucinated responses. We evaluate our approach extensively across several LLMs and established benchmarks. Empirical results demonstrate that our proposed single-pass approach provides consistent improvements over existing attention-based and multi-response baselines across two hallucination-detection benchmarks, while achieving competitive performance across diverse LLM architectures. Further analysis reveals that impaired context sharing among tokens during causal generation is strongly associated with hallucination occurrences in LLMs. In particular, hallucinated responses are consistently characterized by an over-reliance on self-attention, diffused context retrieval from earlier tokens, or information over-squashing, especially in the final transformer layer.

</details>


### [232] [Decoupling Internal Representational Changes and Causal Importance in Fine-Tuned Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21113)
*Lingfang Li,Procheta Sen,Shubham Das,Danushka Bollegala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究微调如何改变大语言模型的内部表征（注意力模式与逐层激活），并考察这些变化是否与EAP识别出的任务相关组件（注意力头、logit级激活）相关联；发现EAP组件集中于特定层，其分布与表征变化最大的层基本无关，且跨任务的EAP组件重叠并不带来跨任务性能迁移，反而在组件高度重叠时导致性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调已成为将LLM适配到各类下游任务的常用方法，但其如何重塑模型内部机制仍缺乏理解。作者希望揭示微调对内部表征（注意力模式、逐层激活）的影响，并检验这些变化是否与驱动任务性能的任务相关组件（由EAP识别）存在联系。

Method: 采用EAP识别驱动任务性能的关键组件（如注意力头和logit级激活），分析微调前后LLM内部表征的变化，包括注意力模式与逐层激活；比较EAP组件在层间的分布与微调中表征变化显著的层；并考察不同任务间EAP组件的重叠程度与跨任务性能迁移/退化的关系。

Result: EAP识别的组件集中在特定层，表明模型内化任务特定行为存在一定程度的功能局部化；这些组件在层间的分布与微调中表征变化最大的层基本不相关；跨任务的EAP组件重叠并不转化为跨任务性能迁移，若任务性质不同（如分类与生成任务）尤其如此；当两个任务在EAP组件上高度重叠时，在一个任务上微调反而可能导致另一个任务性能退化。

Conclusion: 微调带来的表征变化与EAP所识别的任务关键组件在层分布上并不一致；EAP组件的重叠并不等同于可迁移性，任务性质不同时甚至可能引发负迁移与性能退化，提示任务相关组件的共享可能带来干扰而非增益。

Abstract: Fine-tuning has emerged as a widely adopted approach for adapting LLMs to a variety of downstream tasks. However, how it reshapes their internal mechanisms remains poorly understood. To address this, we investigate how fine-tuning alters internal representations in LLMs, including attention patterns and layer-wise activations, and examine whether these changes are linked to task-relevant components identified by EAP (e.g., attention heads and logit-level activations) that drive task performance. We find that EAP-identified components are concentrated within specific layers, indicating a degree of functional localisation in how models internalise task-specific behavior. Notably, the distribution of these components across layers is largely uncorrelated with the layers undergoing the most substantial representational changes during fine-tuning. Furthermore, we observe that overlap in EAP-identified components across tasks does not translate into cross-task performance transfer if the tasks are different in nature (e.g. classification vs. generative tasks). More specifically, fine-tuning on one task can lead to a degradation of performance on another when the two tasks exhibit a high degree of overlap in their EAP-identified components.

</details>


### [233] [TinyCeNN-LM: Quality-Gated Conversion of Pretrained Attention with CeNN-Inspired Cellular-Recurrent Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21139)
*Kabeh Mohsenzadegan,Vahid Tavakkoli,Kyandoghere Kyamakya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 TinyCeNN-LM 的质量门控后训练转换框架，用类 CeNN 的细胞循环层替换预训练语言模型中的注意力层，通过表示保真度与 NLL 双阈值逐层接受或回滚，实现保守的结构转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练模型中替换注意力本质是兼容性问题：看似合理的替代模块可能改变后续层所期望的表示。因此需要一种受质量门控约束的转换方式，而非普遍性的注意力替换或加速。

Method: 采用质量门控后训练转换框架，基于 CeNN 启发的细胞循环层，具备有界局部处理、紧凑循环记忆、路由、融合与接受-回滚验证。研究三种实现：Integrated Memory、MemoryFusion、PDelta3-GDN2-CLVR+Local32。严格 PDelta3 转换仅在表示与 NLL 标准同时通过固定阈值时才接受该层。

Result: SmolLM2-135M 上第 0-2 层被接受，累计 ΔNLL=+0.01209；第 3 层虽 NLL 可接受但表示保真度不达标被拒绝。Qwen3.5-0.8B 上第 3、7、11 层全注意力层被接受，最终 ΔNLL=+0.02073。Integrated Memory 将困惑度控制在 -0.07% 至 +0.93%，总缓存最多减少 6.01%。转换后 Qwen 模型在 200 项下游抽样检查中整体准确率为 28.5%–32.0%。

Conclusion: 结果支持保守的、质量门控的结构转换，而非普适的注意力替换或速度提升。

Abstract: Replacing attention in a pretrained language model is a compatibility problem: a plausible substitute may alter representations expected by later layers. TinyCeNN-LM introduces a \emph{quality-gated post-training conversion} framework using CeNN-inspired cellular-recurrent layers with bounded local processing, compact recurrent memory, routing, fusion, and accept-or-rollback validation. Three implementations are studied: Integrated Memory, MemoryFusion, and PDelta3-GDN2-CLVR+Local32. Strict PDelta3 conversion accepts a layer only when representation and NLL criteria pass fixed thresholds. On SmolLM2-135M, layers 0-2 are accepted with cumulative $Δ\mathrm{NLL}=+0.01209$, while layer 3 is rejected despite acceptable NLL because representation fidelity fails. On Qwen3.5-0.8B, full-attention layers 3, 7, and 11 are accepted with final $Δ\mathrm{NLL}=+0.02073$. Integrated Memory keeps perplexity within $-0.07\%$ to $+0.93\%$ while reducing total cache by up to $6.01\%$. A sampled 200-item downstream sanity check gives $28.5\%$--$32.0\%$ overall accuracy for converted Qwen releases. The results support conservative, quality-gated structural conversion rather than universal attention replacement or speedup.

</details>


### [234] [Clinician-Grounded Quality Assurance for AI-Assisted Psychiatric Intake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21149)
*King Shi,Amanda Li,Jonathan Ivey,Synthia Qia Wang,Guan Gui,Hyunseo Kim,Peter Zandi,Jason Straub,Jacob Taylor,Ananya Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出以记忆增强患者模拟器为核心的临床医生评估平台InterviewPlayground，用于对AI精神科问诊系统进行常规质量保障评估，可在不同问诊风格间比较、降低临床医生负担并衡量临床相关表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI辅助精神科问诊系统投入使用前，医疗系统需要切实可行的手段，按临床质量标准对这些工具进行常规质量保障评估。该评估任务需满足三点：支持跨问诊方式的比较、尽量减少临床医生负担、以及衡量对部署方具有临床相关性的表现。

Method: 作者围绕记忆增强的患者模拟器构建了临床医生主导的评估平台InterviewPlayground，用于开放式AI问诊；利用专家撰写的病例小故事（vignettes）创建可交互患者，搭建模拟问诊平台，并设计贴合问诊场景的评估方式。随后开展6名临床医生、25分钟的试点评估，将LLM问诊者与临床医生进行对比。

Result: 在6名临床医生参与、时长25分钟的试点中，与基于GPT的LLM问诊者相比：LLM找回了更多嵌入病例中的临床相关条目（88.0% vs. 38.9%），但作出了更多并非基于问诊的临床推断（56.8% vs. 27.8%），且更少地对已识别的安全问题进行特征描述（33.3% vs. 66.7%）。

Conclusion: 该平台为该任务的规模化质量保障部署奠定了基础，可在保持临床医生主导评估标准的同时，实现跨问诊风格的比较与临床相关表现的量化测量。

Abstract: Before patients can use AI-assisted psychiatric intake systems, health systems need practical ways to routinely evaluate these tools against their clinical standards for quality assurance. Because clinicians may use different intake styles, evaluation for this task must (1) support comparison across interviewing approaches, (2) minimize clinician burden, and (3) measure clinically relevant performance for health systems deploying these technologies. We present a clinician-grounded evaluation platform built around a memory-augmented patient simulator for open-ended AI interviewing, InterviewPlayground. We created interactive patients using InterviewPlayground with our expert-authored vignettes, constructed a simulated intake platform for the interviews, and designed evaluation modalities relevant to intake. In a pilot of 6 clinicians in a 25-minute assessment compared to a GPT-based LLM intake interviewer, the LLM recovered more of the clinically relevant items embedded in the patient vignettes (88.0% vs. 38.9%), but made more clinical inferences not based on the interview (56.8% vs. 27.8%), and characterized identified safety concerns less often (33.3% vs. 66.7%), setting the stage for deployed quality assurance for this task.

</details>


### [235] [Can Agents Design Better Chips with a Higher Level Abstraction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21157)
*Zijian Ding,Yang Zou,Yizhou Sun,Jason Cong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了四种芯片设计流程，提出结合Agent化HLS与HLS后RTL精修的工作流AHRR，在11个任务的基准上相较直接RTL设计取得2.6倍几何平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM Agent芯片设计方法大多直接面向RTL，未充分利用更高层次抽象带来的设计优势，因此作者探究Agent能否通过高层抽象设计出更好的芯片。

Method: 对比直接RTL设计、Agent化HLS设计、编译器后HLS精修和HLS后RTL精修四类流程，并将Agent化HLS设计与HLS后RTL精修组合为AHRR；以FPGA作为易于部署的端到端评估平台，在11个任务构成的多样化基准上评估。

Result: AHRR在全部基准上相较直接RTL设计取得2.6倍几何平均加速；案例研究表明HLS将设计知识蒸馏为可供Agent利用的抽象，而RTL精修则恢复低层优化机会。

Conclusion: AHRR是一种有前景的Agent化芯片设计工作流，其设计流程权衡在很大程度上与目标技术无关，代码与评估工件已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly being explored for chip design, but most existing approaches operate directly at RTL. We ask whether agents can design better chips by leveraging higher-level abstractions. We compare Direct RTL Design, Agent-based HLS Design, Post-Compiler HLS Refinement, and Post-HLS RTL Refinement, and combine Agent-based HLS Design with Post-HLS RTL Refinement as Agent-based HLS with RTL Refinement (AHRR). We use FPGAs as a practical, easy-to-deploy platform for end-to-end evaluation, but note that the design-flow tradeoffs we study are largely independent of the target technology. Across a diverse 11-tasks benchmark suite, AHRR achieves a 2.6$\times$ geometric-mean speedup over Direct RTL Design across our benchmark suite. Case studies show that HLS distills design knowledge into abstractions that agents can leverage, while RTL refinement recovers lower-level optimization opportunities. Together, these results make AHRR a promising workflow for agentic chip design. The code and evaluation artifacts are available at https://github.com/ZijD/AHRR.

</details>


### [236] [SpecOpt: Contact-Diff Reasoning for Agentic Molecule Optimization Toward Binding Specificity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21165)
*Thao Nguyen,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"特异性优化(SpecOpt)"这一新分子设计任务与配套的智能体框架,通过对已有药物进行受限结构修饰来提升其对目标靶点相对脱靶的结合偏好,并在915个化合物上实现84.8%的成功改善率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脱靶蛋白结合是小分子药物不良反应的主要来源,但现有基于结构的分子设计方法多聚焦于从头生成选择性化合物,而忽视了改进已上市、表征充分的现有药物的选择性。

Method: 构建基于ChEMBL的基准数据集,通过药物机制注释确定预期靶点、通过实测活性确定脱靶;开发智能体框架:将化合物分别对接至目标与脱靶,通过残基感知的原子-蛋白接触比较对接构象,将差异相互作用输入大语言模型以提出针对性结构修饰,候选分子需通过分子相似性、ADMET及靶点-脱靶对接选择性筛选保留。

Result: 在915个化合物上,智能体为84.8%的化合物改善了靶点-脱靶结合差距,将平均差距从-0.72 kcal/mol提升至+0.47 kcal/mol,同时与起始化合物的平均Tanimoto相似度保持0.72。消融实验显示残基特异性接触信息是关键优化信号:将其替换为二值接触指示后,29个消融化合物上均无改善。

Conclusion: SpecOpt被确立为一个独立的分子设计问题,残基感知的差异相互作用是提升现有化合物选择性的有效信号。

Abstract: Off-target protein binding is a major source of adverse effects for small-molecule drugs, yet most structure-based molecular design methods focus on generating selective compounds de novo rather than improving the selectivity of existing, well- characterized drugs. We introduce specificity optimization (SpecOpt), a molecular design task that seeks constrained structural modifications to an existing compound that increase its binding preference for an intended target over known off-targets while preserving its structural identity and drug-like properties. To enable systematic evaluation, we construct a ChEMBL-derived benchmark from compound-target interaction data, identifying intended targets through curated drug-mechanism annotations and off- targets through measured activities. We then develop an agentic framework that docks each compound against its intended target and off-targets, compares the resulting poses through residue-aware atom-protein contacts, and provides these differential interactions to a large language model to propose targeted structural modifications. Candidates are retained only if they satisfy molecular similarity, ADMET, and target-off-target docking selectivity criteria. On 915 compounds, the agent improves the target- off-target binding gap for 84.8% of compounds, shifting the mean gap from -0.72 to +0.47 kcal/mol while maintaining a mean Tanimoto similarity of 0.72 to the starting compounds. Ablation studies identify residue-specific contact information as the critical optimization signal: replacing residue identities with binary contact indicators eliminates improvement on all 29 ablation compounds. These results establish SpecOpt as a distinct molecular design problem and demonstrate residue-aware differential interactions as an effective signal for improving the specificity of existing compounds.

</details>


### [237] [Implicit Rule Induction with Test-Time Task Embeddings in ARC-like Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21181)
*Adrien Deliège,Claas Beger,Marc Van Droogenbroeck,Melanie Mitchell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出VARC中的两步测试时训练协议Embed-TTT：先仅微调任务嵌入，再冻结嵌入微调骨干网络，从而更清晰地分离规则归纳与规则执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARC等基准测试难以判断模型成功是真正推断了任务规则，还是依赖捷径。作者希望区分ARC类任务中的规则归纳与规则执行，以推动更严谨的基准设计。

Method: 在VARC模型（预训练骨干+可训练任务嵌入）基础上，提出两步TTT协议：第一步仅微调任务嵌入（Embed-TTT，参数量不足模型参数的0.01%），第二步冻结该嵌入并微调骨干网络。在ARC-AGI-1、ConceptARC及两个已知规则的受控数据集上进行评估，并结合嵌入检索、线性探针与定性分析。

Result: Embed-TTT持续产生更优的任务嵌入：与真实任务规则对齐更好，提升基于嵌入的检索效果，并可对已知规则进行准确线性探针。仅优化任务嵌入即可解决ARC-AGI-1、ConceptARC和Mini-ARC中相当一部分任务，完整两步流程进一步提升最终性能。Embed-TTT还能恢复参数化规则的几何结构，学习到支持规则插值但无法外推的组合能力。

Conclusion: 任务嵌入层面即可承载显著的规则归纳能力，两步协议有助于在ARC类评估中更清晰地区分规则归纳与规则执行，并提示应设计能区分分布内与分布外规则的基准。

Abstract: The Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus and related benchmarks evaluate whether AI models can solve novel reasoning tasks, but often leave unclear whether success reflects inference of the intended underlying rule or reliance on shortcuts. We address this gap by studying test-time task embeddings in Vision ARC (VARC), a model in which a pre-trained backbone is complemented by a trainable embedding representing the transformation rule. In the original VARC, test-time training (TTT) is jointly applied to the backbone and task embedding. Here we introduce a novel two-step TTT protocol: first finetune only the task embedding (Embed-TTT), then freeze it and finetune the backbone. Across ARC-AGI-1, ConceptARC, and two controlled datasets with known rules, Embed-TTT consistently yields improved task embeddings, ones that align better with underlying task rules, improve embedding-based retrieval, and enable accurate linear probing of known rules. Qualitatively, Embed-TTT identifies more semantically meaningful relations between test and train tasks on ARC-AGI-1. We also show that optimizing only task embeddings (less than 0.01% of model parameters) already solves a non-trivial fraction of ARC-AGI-1, ConceptARC, and Mini-ARC tasks, while the full two-step pipeline improves final performance. Finally, we show that Embed-TTT recovers the underlying geometric structure of parametric rules and learns compositional capabilities that enable rule-wise interpolation, but not extrapolation. These findings support a clearer separation between rule induction and rule execution in ARC-like evaluations, motivating benchmarks that better distinguish in-distribution from out-of-distribution rules.

</details>


### [238] [AI-GRACE: A Use-Case Operationalization Framework for Agentic AI: From Organizational Objectives and Obligations to Deployment Capabilities and Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21192)
*John Cuneo,David Chun,Gaurav Khanna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AI-GRACE 框架，将组织治理与技术实现相连接，针对具体用例明确需要验证、控制和观测的内容，以在满足合规义务的同时实现预期结果，并用虚构的零售银行案例演示该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署代理式人工智能的组织，不能仅判断模型是否可信，还需针对具体用例确定应验证、控制与观测的要素，以交付预期成果并履行适用义务。现有工作缺乏将组织治理与技术实现有效衔接的操作化路径。

Method: 采用设计科学 framing 方法贡献，并以情境方法工程指导情境化裁剪与复用；基于专业观察与对标准及文献的目的性综合，先确立目标与义务，再在七个拟议风险域（含任务与价值实现）中评估风险，进而导出部署前保障、运行时控制与证据要求，用于能力资格认定、差距评估与逻辑架构设计；提出 Agent Operating Envelope 界定许可动作与升级条件，并以 RAIL（风险对齐的独立性等级）概括授权的自主程度。

Result: 构建了 AI-GRACE 框架及其关键构件（七风险域、部署前保障/运行时控制/证据要求、Agent Operating Envelope、RAIL），并通过一个虚构的零售银行应用场景进行方法演示，形成可追溯的决策依据。

Conclusion: 该贡献为组织判断“必须实现什么、已具备什么、仍有哪些未解决”提供了可追溯的基础；其是否提升部署决策质量、效率与复用性仍需实证评估验证。

Abstract: Organizations deploying agentic artificial intelligence must determine more than whether a model is trustworthy; they must establish what to validate, control, and observe for a use case to deliver its intended outcome while meeting applicable obligations. This paper proposes AI-GRACE (Agentic Intelligence-Governance, Risk, Assurance, Controls, and Evidence) as a use-case operationalization framework connecting organizational governance with technical implementation. The proposal draws on professional observations and a purposive synthesis of standards and literature, using design science to frame the method contribution and situational method engineering to guide contextual tailoring and reuse. The framework establishes objectives and obligations and then assesses risks in seven proposed domains, including mission and value realization. It derives requirements for assurance before deployment, runtime controls, and evidence, which guide capability qualification, gap assessment, and a logical architecture. An Agent Operating Envelope specifies permitted actions and escalation conditions, while Risk-Aligned Independence Levels (RAIL) summarize the authorized independence. A fictional retail banking application illustrates the method. The contribution is a traceable basis for deciding what an organization must implement, what it already supports, and what remains unresolved. Empirical evaluation must establish whether it improves deployment decisions, efficiency, and reuse.

</details>


### [239] [Information-Gain Rewards over Diversity-Pruned Tests: GT-Anchored Verifier Co-Training for Reliable Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21208)
*Ana Nunez,Peyman Najafirad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoVer 是一个单策略 GRPO 框架，通过信息增益奖励与多样性感知筛选，同时缓解自博弈代码生成中的宽松性坍塌与集中偏差，在多个基准上显著提升 pass@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自博弈方法让单一语言模型同时充当代码编写者与测试作者，有望使代码生成 RL 摆脱固定测试集的限制，但存在两个耦合的失效模式：宽松性坍塌（permissiveness collapse），即 pass-rate 奖励被琐碎、无区分度的测试所最大化；以及集中偏差（concentration bias），即 i.i.d. 采样的测试聚集于众数输入，抬高估计量方差。

Method: CoVer 采用单策略 GRPO 框架。其一，引入信息增益（IG）奖励，用自生成测试的通过/失败向量与一个分级的、锚定真实标签的正确性信号 y ∈ [0,1]^m 之间的互信息对测试打分，并以二者协方差符号作为门控，仅使具有正向区分度的测试获得奖励。其二，设计三阶段多样性感知选择步骤，将候选池剪枝为行为上非冗余的测试套件（无效性过滤、输入字符串过滤、执行剖面过滤），在固定执行预算下提高 IG 估计量的有效样本量。

Result: 在五个基准（LiveBench、MBPP、LiveCodeBench、CodeContests、CodeForces）上，CoVer 相比 Qwen2.5-Instruct 骨干将 one-shot pass@1 在 7B 上提升 +5.8 点，在 14B 上提升 +7.1 点，并在两种规模下均取得所有对比方法中最高的宏平均。作为 CodeT 排序流程中的即插即用骨干，CoVer-7B 额外带来 +3.5 点的提升。

Conclusion: CoVer 通过联合训练代码生成器与验证器，同时为生成与选择两个环节带来增益，验证了共训练（co-training）的双重收益。

Abstract: Self-play methods that co-train a single language model as both coder and test author promise to move code-generation RL beyond fixed test suites, but they suffer from two coupled pathologies: permissiveness collapse, where pass-rate rewards are maximised by trivial, non-discriminative tests, and concentration bias, where i.i.d. sampled tests cluster on modal inputs and inflate estimator variance. We introduce CoVer (Co-trained Coder and Verifier), a single-policy GRPO framework that addresses both failure modes. First, an information-gain (IG) reward scores each self-generated test by the mutual information between its pass/fail vector and a graded, ground-truth-anchored correctness signal y [0, 1] m, gated by the sign of their covariance so that only positively discriminative tests receive reward. Second, a three-stage diversity-aware selection step prunes a candidate pool to a behaviourally non-redundant suite (invalidity, input-string, execution-profile filtering), raising the effective sample size of the IG estimator at fixed execution budget. On five benchmarks (LiveBench, MBPP, LiveCodeBench, CodeContests, Code-Forces), CoVer raises one-shot pass@1 by +5.8 points at 7B and +7.1 points at 14B over the Qwen2.5-Instruct backbone, and achieves the highest macro-average among all compared methods at both scales. As a drop-in backbone inside the CodeT ranking pipeline, CoVer-7B adds +3.5 points, demonstrating the dual benefit of co-training for both generation and selection.

</details>


### [240] [Ability-Residual Decoupled Modeling for Affective Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21214)
*Boyuan Zhao,Meng Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"能力-残差解耦框架"用于情感认知诊断，通过显式建模认知残差（学生、题目、概念、学生-概念及低秩学生-题目分量），再用情感模块调制猜测/失误效应，Q矩阵约束的概念残差注意力只聚合题目相关概念残差。在四个数据集、六种诊断骨干上验证了预测增益与情感对齐改进，并抑制了"情感污染"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知诊断从作答日志推断概念掌握，但作答还受情绪、投入度、疲劳等非认知因素影响，因此需要情感认知诊断。现有方法假设骨干模型已解释能力、题目、概念效应，剩余误差主要归因于情感；作者指出该假设在真实教育数据中不成立：题目校准偏差、系统性概念偏差、个性化学生-概念偏移和潜在学生-题目匹配会形成稳定的认知残差，若不显式建模，这些残差会泄漏进情感表示，造成情感污染。

Method: 提出能力-残差解耦框架。模型先通过学生、题目、概念、学生-概念以及低秩学生-题目分量捕捉未被建模的认知残差；随后由情感模块调制猜测/失误（guess/slip）效应；并设计受Q矩阵约束的概念残差注意力机制，自适应地仅聚合与题目相关的概念残差，从而将认知残差与情感表示解耦。

Result: 在ASSIST2017、ASSIST2012、ASSIST2009和Junyi四个数据集上，结合六种认知诊断骨干模型，报告的对比中均取得作答预测性能提升；在有情感标签时，情感对齐总体改善。消融实验、泄漏探测、主成分分析可视化、长尾分析和案例研究进一步表明：能力残差吸收了稳定的认知偏差，减少了情感分支中的认知污染，并提升了认知诊断模型的鲁棒性和预测精度。

Conclusion: 显式建模并解耦认知残差是缓解情感认知诊断中情感污染的有效途径，可同时提升响应预测精度与情感表征质量；该方法在多种骨干和多数据集上具备通用性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Cognitive diagnosis infers students' concept mastery from response logs. However, students' responses are not determined by mastery alone: non-cognitive factors such as emotion, engagement, and fatigue can also affect performance. Affective cognitive diagnosis therefore extends conventional cognitive diagnosis by incorporating affective states. Existing methods often assume that the cognitive diagnosis backbone has already explained ability, item, and concept effects, so the remaining errors can be attributed mainly to affect. We argue that this assumption can be insufficient in real educational data: item calibration bias, systematic concept bias, personalized student-concept deviations, and latent student-item matching can form stable cognitive residuals. Without an explicit modeling pathway, these residuals may leak into affective representations, producing affect contamination. To address this problem, we propose an ability-residual decoupled framework for affective cognitive diagnosis. The model first captures unmodeled cognitive residuals through student, item, concept, student-concept, and low-rank student-item components, and then uses an affective module to modulate guess/slip effects. A Q-matrix-constrained concept residual attention mechanism adaptively aggregates only item-relevant concept residuals. Experiments on ASSIST2017, ASSIST2012, ASSIST2009, and Junyi with six cognitive diagnosis backbones show response-prediction gains across the reported comparisons and generally improved affect alignment when affect labels are available. Ablation studies, leakage probes, principal component analysis visualization, long-tail analysis, and case studies further indicate that ability residuals absorb stable cognitive bias, reduce cognitive contamination in the affective branch, and enhance the robustness and predictive accuracy of cognitive diagnosis models.

</details>


### [241] [A Fully Differentiable Neuro-Soft-Symbolic Framework for Perceptual Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21221)
*Hongyan Wei,Wael AbdAlmageed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种可微的神经-软符号框架，将视觉感知与任务规划统一在同一计算图中，通过连续软符号状态与可微软转移算子实现感知不确定性的保留及规划反馈到感知，在Blocksworld等任务上显著超越基线且计算开销更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感知规划任务需要两项关键能力：准确感知不确定场景、按逻辑规则规划有效动作序列。传统方法将感知转化为离散符号事实后再规划，丢弃了感知不确定性，并切断了任务级反馈到感知的通路。

Method: 提出通用的全可微神经-软符号框架，将视觉感知与任务规划置于单一计算图中：维护连续软符号状态，将领域规则提升为可微的软T_P转移算子，在短规划时域上优化动作logits；规划目标的梯度可反向更新感知参数，使任务相关的感知表示在规划过程中被细化。

Result: 在Blocksworld上解决40/40个LatPlan-40任务、596/600个PlanBench-600任务（LatPlan为33/40，推理模型基线为587/600），且计算量与时间显著更少；感知不确定性消融中，成功率由冻结感知的59%提升至83%；进一步在Blocksworld场景进行任务-运动仿真，验证了解码任务计划与下游机器人运动执行的兼容性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将感知与规划端到端可微耦合，可在保留感知不确定性并引入任务级反馈的同时，提升感知规划性能与效率，并具备向机器人运动执行延伸的可行性。

Abstract: Perceptual planning tasks require two key capabilities: accurately perceiving uncertain scenes and planning valid action sequences following logical rules. Conventional methods convert perception into discrete symbolic facts and then plan, discarding perceptual uncertainty and severing task-level feedback to perception. We introduce a generic, fully differentiable neuro-soft-symbolic framework that connects visual perception and task planning within a single computational graph. The framework maintains a continuous soft symbolic state, lifts domain rules into a differentiable soft-$T_P$ transition operator, and optimizes action logits over a short planning horizon. Gradients from the planning objective can also update the perception parameters, allowing task-relevant perceptual representations to be refined during planning. On Blocksworld, our method solves 40/40 LatPlan-40 tasks and 596/600 PlanBench-600 tasks, compared with 33/40 for LatPlan and 587/600 for the reasoning-model baseline, while requiring substantially less computation and time. In the perceptual-uncertainty ablation, our method improves the success rate from 59\% with frozen perception to 83\%. We further conduct task-and-motion simulations on Blocksworld scenes, providing an execution-level validation of the compatibility between decoded task plans and downstream robotic motion execution.

</details>


### [242] [CogGym: Towards Large-Scale Comparative Evaluation of Human and Machine Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21259)
*Lance Ying,Jinzhou Wu,Yingshan Susan Wang,Shivam Aarya,Luca M. Schulze Buschoff,Harry Chen,Katherine M. Collins,Andrea de Varda,Shuhao Fu,Sean Dae Houlihan,Akshay K. Jagadish,Guangyuan Jiang,Samuel Kiegeland,Tetsu Kurumisawa,Rongzhi Liu,Ryan Liu,Ningshan Ma,Kathryn McGregor,Younes Strittmatter,Polina Tsvilodub,Jacob Hoover Vigly,Sarah Wu,Enjie Xu,Yiling Yun,Kelsey Allen,Tyler Brooke-Wilson,Brian Christian,Evelina Fedorenko,Michael C. Frank,Michael Franke,Tao Gao,Samuel J. Gershman,Robert D. Hawkins,Jennifer Hu,Julian Jara-Ettinger,Max Kleiman-Weiner,Sydney Levine,Tal Linzen,Hongjing Lu,Timothy O'Donnell,Desmond C. Ong,Steven T. Piantadosi,Rebecca Saxe,Eric Schulz,Tianmin Shu,Felix A. Sosa,Ilia Sucholutsky,Tan Zhi-Xuan,Tomer Ullman,Fei Xu,Ilker Yildirim,Jian-Qiao Zhu,Thomas L. Griffiths,Tobias Gerstenberg,Kevin Smith,Joshua B. Tenenbaum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CogGym框架，用认知科学实验标准化比较人与模型行为，发现模型越大越接近人类判断，但在常识推理上仍显著低于人类信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI与认知科学都致力于理解并建模人类智能。随着AI能力提升，需弄清模型反应在哪些方面类似人类、在哪些方面系统性偏离。但人类可执行的任务范围极广、种类繁多，使得人与模型之间可扩展且严谨的对比十分困难。

Method: CogGym采用半自动、人在回路的流水线，将多样实验范式标准化为任务无关的Experiment Markup Language（EML），在匹配实验试次上做可复现、保真的规模化比较。首发版本整理并标准化了来自100篇论文的258个认知实验，聚焦人类常识推理，并评测了50个大语言模型。

Result: 模型规模与新旧呈明显缩放趋势：更大更新的模型更能复现人类判断。但模型在常识推理上的进步远慢于数学、编程等形式推理基准；模型与人类的拟合仍远低于人类分半信度（文本R²=0.93、图像0.95、视频0.92），最佳模型仅达文本R²=0.59、图像0.58、视频0.43。

Conclusion: CogGym旨在作为持续演进的评测框架，不断纳入新的认知科学实验，刻画模型行为在何处类人、何处系统性偏离，以及这些模式如何随模型与实验演进而变化。

Abstract: Understanding and modeling human intelligence are parallel goals shared by artificial intelligence (AI) and cognitive science. As AI systems grow increasingly capable, in what ways do model responses resemble human responses, and where do they systematically diverge? The sheer breadth and diversity of the tasks humans can perform and think about pose a challenge for scalable and rigorous comparison between humans and models. We introduce CogGym, a scalable, unified framework grounded in cognitive science for systematically comparing model and human behavior on matched experimental trials. CogGym uses a semi-automated, human-in-the-loop pipeline to standardize diverse experimental paradigms into a task-agnostic Experiment Markup Language (EML), enabling reproducible and faithful comparison at scale. For initial release, we curate and standardize 258 cognitive experiments from 100 papers that focuses on human commonsense reasoning, and evaluate 50 large language models against human responses. We find a clear scaling trend where larger and more recent AI models better reproduce human judgments. Yet AI models' improvement on such common reasoning tasks is considerably slower than the gains observed on formal-reasoning benchmarks like math and coding, and model--human fit remains well below human splithalf reliability ($R^2 = 0.93$ on text, $0.95$ on image, and $0.92$ on video) with the best models achieving $R^2 = 0.59$ on text, $0.58$ on image, and $0.43$ on video experiments. We intend for CogGym to provide a living evaluation framework that continually incorporates new cognitive science experiments to characterize where model behavior resembles human behavior, where it systematically diverges, and how those patterns change as models and experiments evolve.

</details>


### [243] [PlaceReasoner-Beta: Reasoning-Driven Macro Placement and Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21263)
*Qiufeng Li,Chengxuan Wang,Rongqian Chen,Quan Cheng,Yihui Ren,Chia-Tung Ho,David Z. Pan,Tian Lan,Weidong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出PlaceReasoner-Beta，一个由验证器引导的多智能体框架，将宏单元布局从黑盒优化重构为闭环推理问题，并发布开放基准PlaceReasoner-Bench，在布线后时序指标上显著优于传统基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化宏单元布局是VLSI物理设计的核心难题。现有方法主要优化手工设计的代理目标（如估计线长），且通常采用一次性数值优化，难以在统一回路中融合视觉布局上下文、专家设计经验以及下游物理设计反馈。

Method: 提出PlaceReasoner-Beta，一个验证器引导的多智能体闭环框架：视觉语言模型（VLM）规划器基于版图图像、宏单元规格与连接结构生成候选布局；几何验证器强制执行物理合法性与专家布局原则；物理验证器利用早期实现反馈精炼候选；布线后优化器利用最终PPA进一步改进有潜力的布局。同时构建PlaceReasoner-Bench，基于开放RTL设计、EDA工具与技术库，包含8个设计×2种宽高比共16个任务，采用固定版图与I/O分配，仅宏单元位置与朝向可变，并使用布线后PPA与DRC而非布线前代理指标进行评估。

Result: 在全部正方形任务中，PlaceReasoner-Beta取得所有DRC干净方法中的最佳时序，相对于经典基线，1:1宽高比下布线后TNS降低61.2%，2:1下降低53.0%；在多数设计上还缩短了布线线长，尽管从未显式优化该目标。

Conclusion: 在物理设计反馈下对空间结构进行推理，能够超越代理目标优化，提升端到端布局质量，验证了闭环推理范式的有效性。

Abstract: Automated macro placement remains a fundamental challenge in VLSI physical design. Despite decades of research, existing approaches predominantly optimize hand-crafted proxy objectives, such as estimated wirelength, and typically produce placements through one-shot numerical optimization, limiting their ability to incorporate visual layout context, codified design expertise, and downstream physical-design feedback in a unified loop. We present PlaceReasoner-Beta, a verifier-guided multi-agent framework that reformulates macro placement as a closed-loop reasoning problem rather than black-box optimization. A vision-language model (VLM) planner generates candidate placements from the floorplan image, macro specifications, and connectivity structure; a geometric verifier enforces physical legality and expert placement principles; a physical verifier refines candidates using early implementation feedback; and a post-route optimizer further improves promising layouts using final PPA. To enable reproducible evaluation, we introduce PlaceReasoner-Bench, a fully open end-to-end benchmark built from open RTL designs, EDA tools, and technology libraries. It comprises 8 designs at two aspect ratios, yielding 16 tasks with fixed floorplans and I/O assignments, so methods differ only in macro positions and orientations and are evaluated using routed PPA and DRC rather than pre-route proxies. Across the benchmark, PlaceReasoner-Beta achieves the best timing among DRC-clean methods on all square tasks, reducing post-route TNS by 61.2% at 1:1 and 53.0% at 2:1 relative to the classical baseline field. It also shortens routed wirelength on most designs despite never explicitly optimizing it, demonstrating that reasoning over spatial structure under physical-design feedback can improve end-to-end layout quality beyond proxy-objective optimization.

</details>


### [244] [Efficient Benchmarking in Production: A Study of an Evolving LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21267)
*Yining She,Lei Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究生产级LLM智能体在迭代过程中的高效复发评估方法，基于574条历史基准运行数据比较随机采样、历史缓存、固定代表性子集与IRT自适应测试，并在实际部署中采用难度分层固定子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级LLM智能体随版本迭代需要反复评估，但完整智能体基准重跑成本高昂，因此需要研究低成本、可复用的复发评估方案，并总结真实部署经验。

Method: 使用某生产分析智能体574条历史基准运行记录，按时间划分为校准期与留出期；比较随机采样、历史缓存、固定代表性子集和基于IRT的自适应测试；并将难度分层固定子集部署到实际生产中。

Result: 多维2PL自适应测试总体分数保真度最佳：仅执行200题，即完整运行的38.5%，MAE为1.03个百分点；最终部署难度分层固定子集，因运营简单，且能无需重新校准迁移到另外五类智能体，在短至一天的校准窗口下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 自适应测试在保真度上最优，但固定难度分层子集在工程可操作性与跨模型迁移性上更具优势；基于部署经验，论文给出了面向生产智能体复发评估的实用建议。

Abstract: Production LLM agents are evaluated repeatedly as they evolve, but full agent benchmarks are costly to rerun. We study efficient recurring evaluation for a production analytics agent serving tens of thousands of monthly active users and report first-hand deployment experience.
